Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The computability operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 294. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 222 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the computability space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 294 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 222. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^379(S, F) is trivial. QED.
